  ▐ aura  Done. **ANSWER.md** is written with the case's answer plus its one-line justification, and nothing else.

**Diagnosis:** Hypercalcemic (parathyroid) crisis due to primary hyperparathyroidism — acute severe symptomatic hypercalcemia from worsening/recurrent PHPT, precipitated by hypovolemia and AKI.

**Justification:** PTH is inappropriately elevated (253.4) despite severe hypercalcemia (albumin-corrected Ca ≈ 15.9), while all secondary causes were excluded (normal 25-OH D, normal PTHrP, negative SPEP/UPEP); the low anion gap of 5 is fully explained by hypoalbuminemia (corrected AG ≈ 8) plus hypercalcemia itself — a red herring, not a myeloma clue.

Two environment notes for you: no web search service is connected, and web crawls to pubmed/duckduckgo were scope-blocked, so the answer rests on the case data alone — if you want it cross-checked against the published case report, allow those domains with `/scope add pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` (or connect a search engine with `/web`).
